# Ascendance 3.36.21 mechanics audit

This is an incomplete mechanics audit, not release certification. Production and staging were not mutated. All functional requests target a local server with a new temporary persistence directory and isolated browser contexts. No application fixes or deployment changes are included.

## Reproduction

Node 24; npm ci; npx playwright install --with-deps chromium; npm run audit:mechanics. On this cloud machine use CHROMIUM_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium. Run npm test and npm run stability:33621 separately: both declared legacy runners are absent in the baseline checkout. CI deliberately fails when these are missing.

## Current execution

{"UNRUN":100,"passed":31,"failed":5}

Every passing row certifies only its named assertions. XP thresholds are not ability activation coverage; resolver tests are not full card coverage; dispatcher observations are not passes. Exceptions from generic fixtures need reproduction in valid gameplay before they become confirmed card defects.

## Known unresolved IDs

| ID | Investigation |
|---|---|
| EQ013 | Terrain/structure adjacency damage has no generic target model. |
| EQ025 | Stagger Save has no generic resolver; heavy-swing restriction needs separate verification. |
| EQ044 | Advantage and extra full Hero turn do not have authoritative semantics in deterministic Hero combat. |
| EQ055 | No authoritative Rapid Fire magazine/reload model. |
| EQ068 | No generic encounter stance and Ability cooldown refresh model. |
| EQ103 | Travel severity is server-owned; no supported reroll command located. |
| EQ116 | 3.36.13 replaced the obsolete attack-die reroll with deterministic Channel. See dedicated normal/Arcane, once-per-round test; old knownRuleBlocks diagnostic is stale. |
| LT012 | Runtime explicitly refuses use and preserves item/action; Rest is unsupported. |
| LT026 | Runtime explicitly refuses use and preserves item/action; Locked/simple-Trap interaction is unsupported. |
| LT150 | Extra Hero turn is not defined by the room turn protocol. |
| LT159 | Crafting eligibility and material consumption have no shared substitution hook. |

## Remaining coverage gaps

Ability effects at levels 1–16, every branch/trigger/expiry of all 323 cards, all equipment and loot effects, full Enemy AI and targeting matrix, all campaign quests, concurrent reaction timing, and adversarial synchronization remain unverified except for explicit named assertions above. The generic dispatcher sweep must not be substituted for those tests. Staging was not used for mutations. A new-task environment restore and GitHub-hosted workflow execution are not verified here.
